▶ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, API side)
  ▶ templates (22.13, RA-R1-05, BD-10)
    ✔ a template binding a service, diagnosis, medicine or clinician name cannot be saved - service layer and database (37.232528ms)
    ✔ render check then publish: needs the check, needs every configured language, publishes the whole version, retires the old one (251.746715ms)
    ✔ only the admin realm writes templates; staff and patients read them (T-CONF); anonymous reads none (9.097434ms)
  ✔ templates (22.13, RA-R1-05, BD-10) (299.081562ms)
  ▶ send, dedupe, cancel (22.13, 24, BR-08)
    ✔ send binds allowlisted values only: one queued row per channel, a job per row in the same transaction, no address, no text (33.235387ms)
    ✔ free text, unknown names, a service or clinician field, bad links and condition-revealing location names never reach a notification (TC-NT-06) (18.89921ms)
    ✔ dedupe keys prevent double sends: a repeat creates nothing and queues nothing, also under concurrency (52.946119ms)
    ✔ a rolled-back business transaction leaves no notification and no job; a delayed send waits for send_after (59.018375ms)
    ✔ cancel by dedupe-key prefix cancels queued rows of that resource only; sent rows are final (57.977762ms)
    ✔ in-app notices: a patient lists only their own sent notices and marks them read once (18.12154ms)
  ✔ send, dedupe, cancel (22.13, 24, BR-08) (762.744399ms)
  ▶ row-level security and grants
    ✔ app_api: patients see only their own in-app rows; staff (google) and admin see no patient notification; anonymous sees nothing (9.2226ms)
    ✔ inserts: a patient only for themselves, queued only; admin realm only to principals; a worker needs its patients scope (12.455426ms)
    ✔ the application role cannot change content, delete, or touch receipts; receipts and reads are append-only (12.594297ms)
    ✔ table rules: dedupe key without the recipient, recipient kind consistent, values on the allowlist, forward-only status (8.760651ms)
  ✔ row-level security and grants (43.370778ms)
  ▶ merge and export participants (22.16, 24, SBR-16)
    ✔ repointPatient: R rows move to the survivor, K rows stay, counts add up for notifications, receipts and reads; scope is enforced (67.626921ms)
    ✔ exportPart returns the patient's notifications, receipts and reads - no address and no message text exist to leak (5.372884ms)

  ▶ the send job
    ✔ e-mail: renders the published version in the person's language, attaches the .ics, stores the vendor reference and no address or text (93.024392ms)
    ✔ language: the person's language when published, else the default; SMS and push render without a subject/with a title (103.166979ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-08: SMS off and e-mail on - the SMS is suppressed with its reason and the provider is never called; e-mail is sent (24.803911ms)
    ✔ suppression reasons: no address, unknown recipient, inactive patient, no contributor, no template (83.371868ms)
    ✔ in-app notices need no vendor and are sent even when every external channel is off (TC-NT-04) (5.974221ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-04: a failing provider is retried by the queue; the failure is an error CLASS; the last attempt publishes delivery_failed; recovery sends with the same token (78.165416ms)
    ✔ a vendor refusal (4xx) is final: failed, no queue retry, delivery_failed published (14.438969ms)
    ✔ quiet hours defer the send to when they end, restore the attempt count and queue the job (23.229376ms)
    ✔ a template that leaks text is stopped at send time by the output scan; the provider is never called (54.463113ms)
    ✔ a render error (a declared value missing) is a final failure, not a retry (10.3901ms)
    ✔ BE06-F1: a merge that re-points the row during the vendor call - sent, failed and deferred outcomes are still recorded (under the survivor) (39.395536ms)
    ✔ a patient merged while the job waited: the job follows the survivor and sends to the survivor's contacts (on_merged = resolve) (49.129283ms)
    ✔ a staff notice runs under the organisation scope and resolves through the principal contributor (8.858625ms)
    ✔ no address, message text or canary reaches a log line (0.187893ms)
  ✔ the send job (590.204636ms)
